Phone and email are free again; nobody can sign in.
- **Public:** marked suspended (out of directory, search, sitemap, work lists; People still counts it); tuitions hidden with reason `parent_erased` and 301 like hidden-parent tuitions; old tutor addresses 301 to `/browse/tutors?city=<city>` in `proxy.ts` (`erased_tutor_redirects()` holds sha256 hashes of the old addresses only). `npm run test:accounterase`.

### View as without signing in
No migration. `proxy.ts` stamps `x-tm-view-as` (always overwritten or removed; never on the exit route) while a live ticket is in the browser; `lib/viewAsContext.ts` returns the member only when the ticket verifies, the `view_as_sessions` row is open and under 30 minutes, and the real session is the Owner who started it. Then `lib/supabase/server.ts` `createClient()` gives a service-role client whose `auth.getUser()` is the member, with account calls refused; the plain cookie client is `lib/supabase/serverReal.ts` `createRealClient`. Every write is refused by `lib/viewAsGuardCore.ts` through `lib/supabase/fetch.ts` and the browser client (non-GET → 403 without leaving the process; exceptions: the Owner's token refresh, storage read-link signing, the read-only RPC allowlist `VIEW_AS_READ_RPCS` — a new read-only RPC a member page calls must be added there). No session is minted and the member's last sign-in never moves. Known limit: reads run as service role, so a server query relying on RLS alone (no explicit user filter) shows the Owner more than the member sees. `npm run test:viewasreadonly`.

### Parent reviews
Migration 204. Only a parent whose application is at stage Hired on a tuition they posted may review that tutor, once per hire (`reviews.application_id` unique, `can_review_hire()` in the insert policy, no update policy); 1–5 + text, saved `status='pending'`. The Owner approves or rejects at Trust → Reviews (`/admin/reviews`; `SCREEN_ACCESS.reviews` admin + operations read; `/api/admin/reviews/decide` via `refuseUnlessOwnerApproval`; Audit `review.approve` / `review.reject`). Only approved reviews are public, listed by `tutor_public_page()` and counted by `recompute_tutor_rating`. Demos no longer earn a review. No review JSON-LD. `/review` shows a sign-in prompt when signed out. `pf_hired` carries `{review_link}` (`/review?application=<id>`). `npm run test:reviews`.

### Manager role (renamed from Supervisor)
Migration 206 (function body only). Internal value stays `admin_role 'supervisor'`; everywhere shown it reads **Manager** (`ROLE_LABEL`, `roleBadge` for every viewer — it showed "Admin" before, which is why Aqsa Mughal's badge was wrong; her data did not change), the Team picker, the invite email, `SUPERVISOR_OWN_ONLY`. Past Audit rows unchanged; "Project Manager" for Owner/Partner unchanged. Managers see everyone's data in full (`seesEveryone`, `targetScope`, `staffActivity`, `staffSignupsAll`, SQL `sees_all_staff_signups()`), and still change only their own items. Claims stay Owner/Admin. `npm run test:managerview`.

### Tuitions posted, credit picker, fee card
- Team activity → Tuitions posted: `POSTED_COLUMNS` (`lib/tuitionPostingCore.ts`), sorted by Last 7 days desc; former staff one collapsed "Former staff" row, counted in the Team total. `npm run test:tuitionsposted`.
- Member page "Record a bank payment" picker (`pickerOffers` / `pickerCredits` in `lib/bankPaymentLinkCore.ts`, applied on the server by `linkableCredits()`): only unlinked credits (unmatched or held), never matched/ignored, never a PayPro payout, never above Rs 5,000 (`PICKER_MAX_PKR`); matching amount first, then newest; "+ Add a credit" opens the Finance add form inline and returns with the new credit selected. Supersedes, for this picker only, the 21 Oct offering of auto-matched credits (Finance's own "Link to member" still offers them). `npm run test:creditpicker`.
- /pay/choose fee card heading is exactly تصدیق کروانا کیوں ضروری ہے؟ (`FEE_CARD_TITLE`, `text-tm-green-deep`, text-xl heavy — green on the light navy card is 4.21:1, large text only), Urdu first then English. `npm run test:feecard`.

### Name edit, masking, subject cap, address chip, uploads
No migration.
- **Name edit** (`/api/admin/members/name`, `lib/nameEditCore.ts`, `components/admin/NameEditForm.tsx`): tutor edit panel Name section and parent "Edit name" card; reason required; `formatName`; Change history (field `name`), Audit `member.name_edit`, timeline. A tutor with an approved CNIC (`cnic_verified_at`) is renamed by the Owner only ("Check the spelling against the approved CNIC"; others 403 + alert). After a tutor rename the profile-address box is offered (old address redirects).
- **Who acted** is "Tutor — Name" / "Staff — Name" (`lib/actorLabelCore.ts`); a synthetic `@users.tutormint.org` address is never displayed in admin (`shownEmail()`; `npm run test:nosyntheticemail` scans for raw renders). The tutor edit page's WhatsApp is masked with a logged Show (reveal-contact field `whatsapp`).
- **15-subject cap** now also refuses bulk-import rows over 15. Tutors over the cap see `SubjectCapBanner` (dashboard + Settings) and may only remove. Not capped: a member token writing `tutor_subjects` directly (a trigger would break over-cap tutors re-saving Settings). On 27 Oct 2026: 20 tutors over the cap. `npm run test:subjectcapall`.
- **Address chip**: a typed, undecided address is "Waiting for review" (`parentAddressState`, `addressChipLabel`) and appears in the documents-waiting lists.
- **Uploads**: message photos, ad images and refund screenshots share `lib/uploadLimitCore.ts` — shrunk in the browser, 4 MB on client and server (413 with a clear message), failures always say why; a refund screenshot that fails to save stops the refund being recorded. `npm run test:uploadlimits`, `test:addresschip`, `test:nameedit`.

## Hotfix — "Change to Tutor" failed every time; a parent account can be merged into a tutor account (owner, 27 Oct 2026)

No migration. Later-dated than everything above, so under precedence rule 10 it wins where it touches them: **a parent account may be merged INTO a tutor account of the same person** (supersedes "Same type only", 26 Oct). `npm run test:convertmerge`; read-only `scripts/report-mudasir-convert.ts` (replays the change in a rolled-back transaction), `scripts/report-mudasir-merge-preview.ts` (run with `--conditions=react-server`), `scripts/report-merge-schema.ts`.

- **Cause.** `convertAccount()` inserted a `tutor_profiles` row without `email`, which is NOT NULL there (`ensureProfile` writes it). Every Parent → Tutor change for an account with no earlier tutor profile was refused (23502) and the screen said only "That did not save". The insert now writes `profiles.email`.
- **Never silent.** A refused write returns the database's reason with every run of digits removed (`plainDbError` in `lib/convertAccountCore.ts`), logged as `[convert-account] <step> refused: …`; a one-account-per-mobile clash (23505) says so. Before any write, `findConvertClash()` looks for an account of the target type with the same mobile (last 10 digits), CNIC (13 digits) or a two-way link; the preview and the POST then show "This person already has a Tutor account with … : <name> (ID <id>, phone masked)" and **"Merge into that tutor account instead"** (`mergeInsteadHref`), and the change button stays off. A parent's card also has "Already has a tutor account? Its account ID" → the merge screen.
- **Parent → tutor merge** (`mergeKind` = 'parent_into_tutor' in `lib/memberMergeCore.ts`; the tutor is always kept — `suggestKeep`, and keeping the parent is refused). Same owner-only route, password re-check, preview, op log and exact Undo. Moves: the CNIC (number cleared on the parent first, `CNIC_COLUMNS`, CNIC photos; the tutor's own non-approved CNIC photos are paused) when the tutor has no approved CNIC and the parent's is approved or the tutor has none; the home address with its review columns (`ADDRESS_COLUMNS`) when the tutor's is not approved; tuitions (shown "posted as a parent" — derived from `postedAsParent`) and children; the timeline, follow-ups, notes; the staff credit (earliest wins; a moved credit's `member_role` becomes 'tutor', and a copied credit never changes the kept account's role). Blocked when both paid. Approved documents on both sides do not block a cross-type merge. The parent becomes "Merged into <name>", hidden, sessions revoked, and signing in says `MERGED_LOGIN_MESSAGE`. `member_merges.error` is scrubbed with `plainDbError`.
- **Known gap (reported):** a moved address waiting for review does not appear in a review list on a tutor account (the address review list reads parent accounts); `profile_completion` refreshes on the next profile save.

## Withdraw a contact; the Do not use list (owner, 27 Oct 2026)

Migration 207 (additive, dry-run then applied before the code). A person whose phone number was put on tuitions without their consent asks for it to be removed — personal data removed at a person's request (rule 14). Rules pure in `lib/contactWithdrawCore.ts`, I/O in `lib/contactWithdraw.ts`, gate `lib/contactWithdrawGate.ts`. `npm run test:contactwithdraw`; read-only `scripts/report-contact-consent.ts`, `scripts/report-phone-columns.ts`.

- **Trust → Withdraw a contact** (`/admin/withdraw-contact`, `SCREEN_ACCESS.contactWithdraw = []`): the site Owner ONLY — the page redirects anyone else (the Partner included) and every route answers 401 signed out, 403 + the blocked-attempt alert to anyone else. The number is typed in any common format and normalised with `normalisePkMobile`; it travels only in POST bodies and is only ever shown masked.
- **Search** (`POST /api/admin/contact-withdraw/search`): every tuition whose own contact data holds the number (`job_contacts` phone / WhatsApp / social / address, or written in the title / description — any status, staff- or parent-posted), with TM number, title, city, status, poster, tutors applied and tutors who opened the number; accounts holding it (mobile, WhatsApp, second number, tutor profile number); the imported leads (`legacy_parent_profiles`, `legacy_parents`, `legacy_tutors`). A parent account's own tuitions are listed too.
- **Withdraw this contact** (`POST …/withdraw`, reason required, password re-check): each tuition whose own contact data holds the number → status `'hidden'`, `hidden_reason 'contact_withdrawn'` (earlier status kept in `hidden_prev_status`, Featured off), shown to staff as **"Closed: contact withdrawn"**. Through the hidden-tuition machinery of migration 198 it 301s to `/tuition-jobs/<city>` (or Browse tuitions), is noindex with no JobPosting, is out of every list and the sitemap, and its applications read "Tuition no longer available" (out of To do, the Paid tutors red rules and every badge; the tutor sees "This tuition is no longer available", no reason). The number is removed from `job_contacts` and scrubbed from the tuition text ("[number removed]") — reveals are read live from those, so tutors who opened it no longer see it — and cleared from the imported leads. Migration 207 changed `sync_parent_tuitions` so an account-flag change never un-hides or re-labels a withdrawn tuition. **Accounts are never changed**: when a parent account owns the number the results offer that account's "Close account and erase data" (`/admin/users/<id>#erase`). The Indexing API is not used (a redirecting page is never sent; Google sees the 301 on its next visit).
- **Do not use list** (`GET/POST …/list`): hash + masked form only; removal is Owner only with a reason and the password re-check, stamped, never deleted. `doNotUseRefusal()` (fails open if the list cannot be read) refuses a listed number with "This number has asked not to be contacted. Please don't use it." in `createJob` (the parent's own number or one in the text), `createTeamJob` / `updateTeamJob` (contact number, WhatsApp, text, social), `updateJob`, `saveTuitionContactNumber`, bulk import (`doNotUseChecker`, mobile and WhatsApp), and the two admin mobile changes (`/api/admin/members` change-mobile, `/api/admin/tutors/edit` set-mobile).
- **Audit:** `contact.withdraw` (target the withdrawal row; reason, tuition ids, refs, counts), `job.close_contact_withdrawn` per tuition, `contact.do_not_use_remove` — never a number or its mask (`auditDetailSafe`).
- **Consent report** on the same page: team-account tuitions with a parent number that belongs to no account, counted per staff member (`consentCounts`). On 27 Oct 2026: 1,346 of 1,482.


## Parent consent for listings, team-posted tuition labels, report fixes, payment Submit, tutor address review (owner, 27 Oct 2026)

Migration 208 (additive, dry-run then applied before the code). Later-dated than everything above, so under precedence rule 10 it wins where it touches them: **every staff save of a tuition (create AND edit) needs the "Parent agreed to be listed on TutorMint" tick and a method**; **while the Owner's switch is on, a tuition whose consent is Not recorded never reveals its parent's number to a tutor** (supersedes "View number" for those tuitions, and any earlier reveal); **a staff-posted tuition names its poster "Posted by TutorMint team for a parent ✓"** (supersedes the "Verified tuition" pill on the card and page); **a report on a staff-posted tuition is "Problem with this job" and names nobody**; **/pay/choose's receipt button is "Submit" and the "WhatsApp us" button is gone**. Rules pure in `lib/tuitionConsentCore.ts`, `lib/teamPosterCore.ts`, `lib/reportCardCore.ts`; I/O in `lib/tuitionConsent.ts`. `npm run test:consent`; read-only `scripts/report-consent-baseline.ts`.

- **Consent record** (`tuition_consents`, see Canonical tables). Backfill on 27 Oct 2026: 1,524 tuitions — 1,483 staff-posted Not recorded, 41 parent-posted Recorded (parent posted it themselves). `consentFor(jobIds)` reads it (a missing row reads Not recorded — the safe side).
- **Staff Post a tuition / admin edit** (`components/forms/ConsentBox.tsx` in the shared form's staff block): tick + "How did they agree?" (Phone call / WhatsApp / In person) + optional note. `createTeamJob` and `updateTeamJob` refuse without them (`consentInputProblem`) before any write, then `recordConsent()` (Audit `job.consent_record`). The edit page pre-ticks a recorded consent. **There is no tuition bulk import** (the only import is tutors); `importRowConsent()` is the rule a future one must use (no consent column / empty → Not recorded).
- **"Consent not recorded" chip** (`components/admin/ConsentChip.tsx`) on admin tuition cards and Applications group headers: **Ask parent** → `GET /api/admin/jobs/consent/ask` (the editable `consent_ask` template, Team inbox → Templates → Consent; number added on the server; Audit `job.consent_ask`), **Parent agreed** → `POST /api/admin/jobs/consent` (`SCREEN_ACCESS.tuitionConsent`; a Manager only on own tuitions), and "Parent said no? Withdraw the contact" (link for the Owner; others are told to ask the Owner).
- **Owner switch** Admin → Settings → Privacy (`/admin/settings/privacy`, `SCREEN_ACCESS.consentPrivacy = []`, `POST /api/admin/settings/hide-numbers`, Owner + fresh password, Audit `settings.hide_unconsented_numbers`), ON by default; an unreadable value reads ON. When on, for Not recorded tuitions: `revealJobContact` refuses before any check or spend (no reveal counted), `peekTuitionContact` returns nothing (earlier reveals hidden), `jobContactRevealStatus` answers reason 'consent', `lib/jobFeed` drops the teaser and sets `contactHidden`, and the card / page show "Apply — our team will share your profile with the parent" in place of the number and the reveal buttons. Applying is unchanged (it still uses one unit of the shared monthly pool as an application). Public pages show only the masked teaser, never a number, either way.
- **Team activity → Tuitions posted** gained "Consent recorded" (% of that staff member's posts with consent recorded — last 7 days and all time; `countConsent`), summed in the Team total.
- **Poster label.** The team account's stored name is "TutorMint Admin"; it is never shown: the card and page pill read `TEAM_POSTER_LABEL`, messages and report cards use `TEAM_MEMBER_NAME` ("TutorMint team"), the thread header adds the label. JSON-LD unchanged.
- **Reports.** `ReportButton teamJob` offers `JOB_PROBLEM_REASONS` (no response, already filled, wrong details, other); `/api/reports` decides "staff-posted" from the row (`isTeamJob`) and stores `reported_id = null` (`jobReportDecision`). Migration 208 pointed the one existing job report that named the team account at the tuition only. Report cards (`components/admin/ReportTargetBlock.tsx`) link the exact tuition (TM number + title, admin + public page) or profile, and show the reporter's status (tutor: fee paid / Verified badge; parent: verified / mobile) and their applications on that tuition with forward status.
- **/pay/choose**: `SUBMIT_LABEL` "Submit", `bg-gray-200 text-slate-700` and disabled until a receipt is attached, then `bg-tm-green-deep text-white` (pairs in check:contrast). The "WhatsApp us" button is removed from every state; nothing else on the page changed.
- **Tutor home addresses** (`tutorAddressesAwaitingReview`, `loadParentDocs(id, { allowTutor })`, `ParentDocumentReview addressOnly`): a tutor's typed, undecided address is in the Documents to approve list (Overview, People badge) and on the member page with the same Approve / Reject and chip; deciding it never makes a tutor a "verified parent". The waiting rule now also counts a typed address with no status yet, for parents too.

## Hotfix — parent numbers back for tutors; no consent wording on public pages (owner, 27 Oct 2026)

Migration 209 (applied before the code). Later-dated than "Parent consent for listings…" (same day), so under precedence rule 10 it wins where it touches it: **the Owner's switch "Hide parent numbers when consent isn't recorded" is OFF, and OFF is the default** (supersedes "ON by default; an unreadable value reads ON"); **tutors and the public are never told anything about consent**. `npm run test:consent`.

- **Switch.** Migration 209 set `app_settings` 'privacy.hide_unconsented_numbers' = 'false' and wrote one `settings.hide_unconsented_numbers` Audit row as the Owner, reason "Turned off at the owner's request". `parseHideSwitch` is on only for 'true' / 'on' / '1' / 'yes'; `hideNumbersSwitchOn()` reads a missing value, no service client or a failed read as OFF. With it off, the reveal button, reveals on every tuition, the shared pool counting and earlier reveals are exactly as before da9ca744.
- **Wording.** `HIDDEN_CONTACT_LINE` ("Apply — our team will share your profile with the parent") is deleted; the job card and tuition page draw nothing in its place. If the Owner ever turns the switch on, a hidden tuition simply shows no contact line, and a direct reveal answers "The number for this tuition is not available." (`HIDDEN_REVEAL_ERROR`).
- **Kept, admin only:** the consent box on staff Post a tuition / edit, the "Consent not recorded" chip with Ask parent and Parent agreed, the Team activity consent column, "Posted by TutorMint team for a parent ✓", the report fixes, the payment page Submit button.
- **Where the switch lives:** sidebar Settings → Privacy, `/admin/settings/privacy` (Owner changes it; the Partner views). There is no `/admin/settings` index page, so that bare address is a 404.


## Consent not required; pause on complaint; PayPro settlement; email savings (owner, 27 Oct 2026 — later than "Parent numbers back")

Migration 210 (additive, dry-run then applied before the code). Later-dated than everything above, so under precedence rule 10 it wins where it touches them: **parent consent is not needed** (supersedes "Parent consent for listings" — the consent box, the "Consent not recorded" chip with Ask parent / Parent agreed, and the Team activity consent column are hidden; no save requires consent); **"Withdraw a contact" offers "Pause all their tuitions" (default, reversible) beside "Remove number permanently"**; **a tuition paused on complaint does not 301** (the one hidden tuition that stays at its URL); **"New payment received" is not emailed; staff alerts are in the bell**; **every email passes the saving rules in `lib/email/policyCore.ts`**; **a tutor's first application stage reads "Application received"**. `npm run test:complaintemail`; read-only `scripts/report-paypro-settlement.ts`, `scripts/report-paypro-payouts.ts`, `scripts/report-email-usage-day.ts`.

- **Consent hidden, data kept.** `CONSENT_UI_ENABLED = false` (`lib/tuitionConsentCore.ts`) gates the box (`PostTuitionForm`), the chip (`JobRow`, Applications groups), the Team activity column (`POSTED_COLUMNS` no longer lists it; `POSTED_CONSENT_COLUMN` keeps the word), the consent report on Withdraw a contact, and the Ask parent route (410). `createTeamJob` / `updateTeamJob` record a consent only when one is given. `tuition_consents` and the Privacy switch (OFF, Owner only) are untouched. `admin_message_templates.active` (default true): `consent_ask` is inactive and its Templates group is not shown.
- **Pause on complaint** (rules `lib/contactWithdrawCore.ts`, I/O `lib/contactWithdraw.ts` `pauseContact` / `unpauseContact` / `loadHoldList`; routes `/api/admin/contact-withdraw/pause` and `/unpause`, Owner only via `ownerContactGate`, reason + fresh password, Audit `contact.pause_complaint` / `contact.unpause_complaint` / `job.pause_contact_complaint` / `job.unpause_contact_complaint`, never the number). Each tuition whose own contact data holds the number: status 'hidden', `hidden_reason 'contact_complaint'` ("Paused: contact complaint"), Featured off, its exact earlier state in `contact_hold_jobs`. So it is out of Browse, search, the sitemap and Google Jobs (every reader lists open/paused only); `hidden_tuition_redirects()` leaves it out, so the page stays at its URL and renders only `components/TuitionNotAvailable.tsx` ("This tuition is not available", noindex, no JobPosting, no text); `loadTuitionTarget` refuses any hidden tuition, so no reveal ever shows the number; applications read "Tuition no longer available" (the hidden-tuition machinery). `sync_parent_tuitions()` never un-hides or re-labels it; the 20-day sweep and resume paths act on open/paused only. The number goes in `contact_holds` (hash + masked); `doNotUseRefusal` / `doNotUseChecker` refuse a held number with `HOLD_MESSAGE` ("This number has a complaint on file. Please check with the owner.") — Do not use wins. **Unpause all** writes each snapshot back exactly (only rows still `contact_complaint`), re-runs `sync_parent_tuitions` for their parents, and lifts the hold. "Remove number permanently" is the old withdraw and also ends a live hold (`released_how 'removed'`).
- **PayPro settlement** (`lib/payproSettlementCore.ts` pure, `lib/payproSettlement.ts`; `/admin/finance/paypro`, Finance access; card `components/admin/PayproSettlementCard.tsx` on the Finance page): every approved PayPro payment, fee = amount − MerchantShare from the current export ("Check with PayPro" when the share is above the amount; the usual fee for that amount, "estimated", when the order is not in an export). Payouts = Finance credits recorded as PayPro payout (`bank_transfers.gateway 'paypro'`) whose sender names PayPro, less "Ignore (not a sale)"; one recorded as a PayPro payout from another sender is listed for the Owner and not counted. Allocated oldest first. Refunds stay in the totals (PayPro still pays them out) and are listed. Figures on 27 Oct 2026: 29 payments, gross Rs 5,771, fees Rs 129 (6 "Check with PayPro"), net expected Rs 5,642, received Rs 4,669, pending Rs 973 over 6 payments, oldest unsettled 22 Oct 2026; 1 credit of Rs 10,000 recorded as a PayPro payout but not from PayPro.
- **PayPro wording.** Payments → Settings shows `PAYPRO_RETIRED_LINE` in place of "PayPro mode" (the stored setting is unchanged); Payment gateways has no Active gateway chooser, no PayPro way to pay and no Pay later; the old alert-address form is replaced by a link to Notification settings.
- **Payment emails** (`lib/payments/paymentAlerts.ts`, `lib/staffAlerts.ts`): every staff alert rings the bell (`kind 'staff_alert'`) for active staff whose role opens its screen; "New payment received" is not emailed; "Bank transfer waiting for approval" is emailed to the Owner account's own address (`ownerEmail()` — read from the profile, never in code); the other alert emails are off unless the Owner switches them on (then to the Owner only). The Monday blog suggestions also ring the bell.
- **Notification settings** (`/admin/settings/notifications`, `SCREEN_ACCESS.notificationSettings = []`, `/api/admin/settings/email-types`, Owner + fresh password, Audit `settings.email_type` / `settings.email_daily_limit`): one switch per type in `app_settings 'email.type.<id>'`. Defaults (`defaultOn`): staff alerts off except the bank-transfer one; member emails on; password reset, email confirmation and account erase confirmation always on (`LOCKED_ON`). Also the daily limit (`email.daily_limit`, default 100).
- **Saving rules** (`decideAll` in `lib/email/policyCore.ts`; facts `lib/email/policy.ts`, fail open; applied in `lib/email/send.ts` to `sendOne` and `sendBulk`): skip an empty or `@users.tutormint.org` address, a switched-off type, a Do not email address, an unconfirmed address (except the confirmation email); critical (`confirm_email`, `password_reset`, `account_erased`, `bank_transfer_pending`) always go; same template to the same person at most once in 24 hours; 3 unopened of a type stops that type (only while open tracking is active: `email.open_tracking = 'on'` AND `RESEND_WEBHOOK_SECRET` set); `welcome`, `tuition_match`, `tuition_paused`, `message_digest` are held for one daily summary per person (`daily_summary` template); from 90% of the daily limit (UTC day, Resend's) non-critical emails are held for the next day. Held emails are `email_queue` rows and `email_sends` status 'queued'; `drainEmailQueue()` (daily cron, last step) sends them and clears their bodies. No log line carries an address.
- **Resend webhook** `/api/webhooks/resend`: OFF (503) until `RESEND_WEBHOOK_SECRET` is set; Svix signature checked (`lib/email/webhookCore.ts`), 401 otherwise; `email.bounced` / `email.complained` → `email_do_not_send` (hash + masked), `email.opened` → `email_sends.opened_at`. `/api/internal/email-tracking` (CRON_SECRET) reads Resend's open-tracking state for the domain, read only.
- **Email usage** (`/admin/settings/email-usage`, Owner only): per UTC day and type, sent / skipped (reasons) / failed / held, last 30 days, from `email_sends` (`lib/email/usageCore.ts`). Supabase Auth's own emails are not in this log. 27 Oct 2026 (UTC): 112 sent — 74 tuition paused, 21 confirmations, 8 bank-transfer alerts, 8 new-payment alerts, 1 re-upload alert.


## Payment auto-linking, parent WhatsApp messages, WhatsApp link audit, no wasted tuition emails (owner, 27 Oct 2026)

Migration 211 (additive, dry-run then applied before the code). Later-dated than everything above, so under precedence rule 10 it wins where it touches them: **a bank credit in the Fee match range (default Rs 199–210) counts as a Verification Fee payment and the extra is "rounding"** (supersedes the exact-amount-only receipt match); **a fee-range credit from the same name within 48 hours is matched BEFORE the blind "same amount within 2 days" guess**; **"Tutors applied" is a parent message too** (supersedes "waiting on us — no message"; it is still out of the badge); **every WhatsApp link on the site is built by lib/waLinkCore** (supersedes the fixed-English wa.me member links of 23 Oct). `npm run test:feematch`; read-only `scripts/report-paused-email-recipients.ts`, `scripts/report-fee-autolink-debug.ts`; data step `scripts/dataop-fee-autolink.ts`.

- **Rules** pure in `lib/feeMatchCore.ts` (range, rounding, names — case, spacing, dots and prefixes such as Muhammad / M. / Syed ignored —, 48-hour window, autoLinkPlan, approvalPick, partials, re-label); I/O in `lib/feeMatch.ts`. `buildBoard` takes `feeRange` (loadAll reads the setting) and runs the pass `FEE_NAME_HOW` after "same amount and sender name" and before the blind guess — that guess had taken tutors' payments for someone else's Rs 199 credit while their own Rs 200 sat unmatched.
- **Automatic links are stored** (`runFeeAutoLink`): after a credit is added by hand (`addBankCredits`) or by the bank email reader, and in the one-time clean-up. A pair the board matched by `FEE_NAME_HOW`, or a free in-range credit whose sender name matches an open fee payment within 48 hours, becomes a 'receipt' link noted "Automatic fee match: TM-…" (shown as automatic, not "by hand") and `payments.rounding_pkr` is set. Same range and time but a different name → a one-tap suggestion on Finance ("Link to <member>'s payment"), never a link. Nothing here approves, activates or grants anything.
- **Approving a receipt** (`/api/admin/payments/decide`) attaches the closest unlinked in-range credit within 48 hours (no name needed) and the toast names it.
- **"This member has already paid this fee"** returns the existing fee payment: no credit yet → "Link this credit to that payment" (`bank-link` action `link_existing`); it has one, or was PayPro → "Mark as duplicate — refund due" (`/api/admin/payments/refund-due`, `refund_dues`). A refund due is a Needs-attention item and a row on the member's Payments box until "Mark as refunded".
- **Partial** — a credit below the range is "Partial — Rs X short" with the likely member (one name match among unpaid tutors and open fee payments); `linkDecision` refuses it for the fee: it never activates.
- **Re-label** (Finance credit ⋯ menu): PayPro payout · Bank transfer from a member · Owner / partner transfer (not revenue); Owner only, reason required, `settlement.relabel` in Audit. An owner / partner transfer is never matched and `ignoredCreditIds()` drops it (and refund-due credits) from every money total and PayPro settlement.
- **Fee match range** — Finance → Settings, Owner only with the password, `settings.fee_match_range`.
- All new routes are Owner only (`refuseUnlessOwnerApproval` behind `SCREEN_ACCESS.finance`); the Partner views.
- **Parents** (`lib/parentWhatsApp.ts` `sendParentMessage`): every WhatsApp button to a parent — Follow-ups → Parents, the member page, People, Overview cards, the tuition admin page (parent-posted tuitions) — picks the message by `parentMessageSituation` (the Follow-ups situation, else the nearest), from Team inbox → Templates → Parents (six: `pf_tutors_applied` new, plus `{signin_link}` / `{repost_link}` in email-unconfirmed / expired), always with a TutorMint link and the sender's ref, recorded as a follow-up (source 'parents') with the same Undo, 3-day wait and 3-message rules. `/api/admin/whatsapp` POST returns `{ href, followUpId }` for `components/admin/AdminWhatsAppLink.tsx` (the toast's 10-second Undo); GET still works as a link. No emoji in parent messages. The "Do you need any help…" fallback (test or staff-linked accounts only) now has a link and no emoji. Note: the member-page link had already been api.whatsapp.com since 23 Oct; the "�" the owner saw is not reproducible from the link we build.
- **WhatsApp links**: `waChatHref` (a number, no message) and `waShareHref` (share to anyone) join `waSendHref`; no file outside lib/waLinkCore.ts may contain wa.me/ or api.whatsapp.com (test).
- **No wasted tuition emails** (`lib/notify/tuitionEmailCore.ts`, applied in `prepareEmail`): a `tuition_*` email never goes to the team account, staff, a test / seed / hidden account or a placeholder address. On 26 and 27 Oct 2026 all 56 + 74 "tuition paused" emails went to the team account.
- **Applications**: the tutor's "My applications" pages read `TUTOR_STAGE_TEXT.applied` ("Application received") — they said "Awaiting the parent".

## Simple-English templates with Owner approval; public /post-tuition (owner, 28 Oct 2026)

Migration 212 (additive, dry-run then applied before the code). Later-dated than everything above, so under precedence rule 10 it wins where it touches them: **every member-facing template has a proposed simple-English wording that goes live only when the Owner approves it**; **parent "post a tuition" links go to the public /post-tuition page, never a sign-in or dashboard address**; **Withdraw a contact hides its two buttons for a number already on the Do not use list**. `npm run test:templaterewrite`, `test:posttuitionpage`, `test:withdrawlisted`; read-only `scripts/report-live-templates.ts`, `scripts/report-template-rewrite.ts`.

- **Proposals in code, decisions in the database.** `lib/templateProposals.ts` (43 WhatsApp / Team inbox templates) and `lib/notify/emailWordingCore.ts` (27 member emails — a welcome for a tutor and for a parent are two) hold the proposed wording. Not rewritten: `city_hiring_post` (a public social post), `consent_ask` (switched off), staff emails (payment / SMS / owner alerts, content digest, staff invite) and the two wrappers (an admin's own Team message, the daily summary). Supabase Auth's own emails (sign-up confirmation, password reset) are set in the Supabase dashboard, not here.
- **Writing rules** (`checkWording`, enforced by the test, the review page and the server on every approval): greeting by first name; at most 3 lines (≤140 characters) before the link; exactly one link placeholder, alone on its line and last (a message whose one action is a reply or an email has none — `noLink`); then `Need help? WhatsApp {support_whatsapp}` as the last line; no emoji; never "dashboard", "onboarding", "listing", "verification flow" or "ref"; no price except `fu_fee_reply` (receipts state the amount through `{amount}`, filled at send time); no superlative; no reply-plus-link; only placeholders every send path of that template fills; the Urdu part (`lib/templateUrdu.ts`, generated byte-for-byte from the live rows) unchanged.
- **Every send path fills the new placeholders** through `lib/templateFill.ts` `completeCommon` ({first_name}, {link}/{continue_link}/{pay_link}, {support_whatsapp}): the Team inbox compose box (link per template and role — `lib/composeLinks.ts`), My sign-ups and Stuck follow-ups (`followUpText` — "Still to do" goes just above a link that sits on its own line), `fillPaymentNotFinished`, `fillDocumentsApproved`, the seeded-tuition message, the status image message (`STATUS_PLACEHOLDERS` gained support_whatsapp), `buildForwardMessage` (`supportWhatsapp`). These fill nothing a live template uses differently, so live messages are unchanged until a wording is approved. `SignupsClient` is unused and was not touched.
- **Team inbox → Templates → Review new wording** (`/admin/inbox/review`, `SCREEN_ACCESS.templateReview = []`: the Owner decides, the Partner views; `/api/admin/templates/review` refuses anyone but the Owner): per template its name, recipient, live text, proposed text and a preview with sample data, then Approve / Edit then approve / Keep current; "Approve all" (confirm) approves every undecided one; each template's earlier wording with Restore. Approving keeps the replaced wording in `template_history` first; WhatsApp / Team templates are written to `admin_message_templates.body`, emails to `email_wordings`. Audited `template.review_approve` / `template.review_keep` / `template.review_approve_all` / `template.restore`; the regular editor's saves also keep history (`action 'edit'`). The built-in code defaults ("Reset to default", the empty-row fallback) are still the OLD wording.
- **Emails** go through `lib/notify/emailWording.ts` `renderEmail` → `emailPick.ts` `pickEmail`: an approved wording (read at most once a minute) via `renderWording` (the {link} line becomes the button, the support line sits under it; essential flag and footer unchanged), else the built-in email. `tuition_paused` gained an optional `href` (the tuition's own page).
- **/post-tuition** (`lib/postTuitionPage.ts`): heading "Post your tuition for free", three steps, one button — a signed-in parent goes to the post form, everyone else to sign-up with Parent selected and the form as the return-to. og:title "Post your tuition for free | TutorMint", og:image `/og/post-tuition` (1200×630 PNG), in the pages sitemap. `parentWhatsApp` postLink, My sign-ups' post-a-tuition link, the parent welcome and account-type emails point here.
- **Withdraw a contact**: a searched number already on the Do not use list shows "Already withdrawn on <date> by <name>" and no action buttons (`lib/withdrawListedCore.ts`), with the Owner-only "Remove from Do not use list" as before.

## Content queue from real searches, Claude drafts, female jobs pages, AI visibility, Job Posting fields, indexing order (owner, 28 Oct 2026)

Migration 213 (additive, dry-run then applied before the code). Later-dated than everything above, so under precedence rule 10 it wins where it touches them: **the content queue leads with topics from real Google searches** (Search Console API, or the owner's uploaded Queries CSV); **no priority number is shown anywhere** (the column still orders the "other topics" internally); **the weekly content email goes to the Owner only** (it is still off by default under Notification settings; the bell always rings); **the Indexing API never sends a notice for a tuition that is not open** (supersedes "paused → URL_UPDATED, closed/hired/merged → URL_DELETED" of 12 and 16 Oct — those notices are now marked `not_open`); **sitemap files are not held at the edge** (`cache-control: no-store`, supersedes s-maxage=600); **the female tuition-jobs page matches `gender_preference='female'` OR (no preference AND the whole word "female" in title/description)**; **every JobPosting carries employmentType**. `npm run test:contentsearch`, `test:jobpostingfields`, `test:femalepages`, `test:aivisibility`, `test:ibanlink`, `test:resetapproved`.

- **Search topics** (`lib/contentQueue/searchTopics.ts` pure; `searchTopicsServer.ts` I/O; reader `fetchSearchConsoleQueries` in `gsc.ts`). Queries with 3+ impressions in 28 days, branded ("tutormint", "tutor mint") left out. Tutor-side searches (jobs, vacancies, teaching…) group by city, area, female, online, school; parent-side by their words. Tutor-side first, then by searches. A topic a page answers (the city / female / online / area tuition-jobs page with 3+ open, a tutor landing page, or a published post with the same words and city) is "Already covered by <page>" and never offered; a school-jobs search or a search with no city is not answered by a city page. The rebuild reads the API first (stores the error and keeps the earlier rows when it fails); up to 30 uncovered topics (source `gsc`) head the queue, the other sources fill the rest. Upload: Content queue → "Upload Queries CSV" (`SCREEN_ACCESS.contentQueueUpload = []`, Owner; audited `content_queue.upload_queries`, row count only). The page shows the access status and, when it fails, the owner's steps (`gscAccessProblem`).
- **Weekly email** (`lib/contentQueue/digestCore.ts`): 3 topics, each "N searches last month" + one plain line; Google topics still open in the queue first, then searches made on TutorMint.
- **"Draft this post"** (`POST /api/admin/blog/queue/draft`, `SCREEN_ACCESS.blogQueueDraft` = Owner, Managers, Admin; `lib/blogQueueDraft.ts`, rules `lib/blogQueueDraftCore.ts`): the editor's own machinery (buildBlogBrief, outline, sections in parallel, sanitizeDraft, self-check), saved as status 'draft', not reviewed, not approved, with a Live jobs block and meta title ≤ 60 / description ≤ 155; the topic is marked drafted. Rate-limited on `ai_blog`; audited `blog.draft_from_queue` with model, words, sections, links, timings and checker counts — never text. Model = the existing `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` setting.
- **Live jobs block** (`lib/liveJobsCore.ts`): a post line `{{live-jobs}}` (or `{{live-jobs:female}}` / `{{live-jobs:online}}`) renders up to 6 current open tuitions for the post's city / subject / level (`OpenTuitionsBlock` with `limit`, `tag`, `live`) and "See all" to the city's tuition-jobs page; a post with the block does not repeat the 3 cards at its end. Editor toolbar "Live jobs".
- **Female pages** (`lib/femaleJobsCore.ts`): title "Female Teaching Jobs in <City> — Home Tuition & School Jobs | TutorMint" as given (over 60 characters on purpose); 3+ open matching = indexable and in the sitemap; the footer Tutors column lists up to 4 indexable female pages.
- **AI visibility**: `/llms.txt` gained /post-tuition, city tuition pages and a Costs line pointing at /membership-plans (no amounts); the FAQ's first group is "How TutorMint works" (`#how-it-works`) with "Is TutorMint free to join?". robots.txt has one `User-agent: *` allow rule — every AI crawler (OAI-SearchBot, ChatGPT-User, GPTBot, PerplexityBot, ClaudeBot) is allowed; unchanged.
- **Job Posting fields** (`lib/jobFieldsCore.ts`, `lib/postcodes.ts`): staff create/edit refuse a missing employment type, a missing or 0 fee (band, or the new "Exact amount" → min = max), a missing area, or a postal code that is not 5 digits; parents keep them optional. JSON-LD: employmentType always (stored, else Home/Online → PART_TIME, else FULL_TIME), baseSalary only with a fee, postalCode = stored ?? lookup. Admin "Fee missing" chip on open tuitions with no fee. No fee was invented.
- **Indexing order** (`noticeSendable`, `noticeTier`, `touchedAt` in `lib/googleIndexingCore.ts`; `planDrain(..., facts)`): open tuitions touched (posted, resumed, refreshed, bumped, or an edit notice) in the last 7 days first, newest first; then other open tuitions, newest first. Not open, expired (past 20 days, not swept), merged or at an old address → marked, never sent.
- **Payment page** (`/pay/choose`): the IBAN row is hidden behind "Your app asks for IBAN? Tap here" under the account number. **Reset to default** in Team inbox → Templates restores the approved wording (`lib/templateResetCore.ts`); a template never reviewed, or kept, resets to the built-in default.

## Speed, search words, Locations clean-up, Share on WhatsApp (owner, 28 Oct 2026)

Migration 214 (one template row, dry-run then applied before the code). Later-dated than everything above, so under precedence rule 10 it wins where it touches them: **every admin list loads 25 at a time with "Load more", filtered, searched and counted on the server** (supersedes People's 48-at-a-time client grid, Applications' client-side tabs/search/counts, Tuitions' 40, Locations' 40); **"online", "home", "evening"… are search filters, not unknown words**; **when the typed words name a different subject from the dropdown, the typed search wins**; **"Share on WhatsApp" opens a ready message, not the bare link**; **the female jobs title is "Female Teaching Jobs in <City> | TutorMint"** (supersedes the longer 28 Oct title). `npm run test:sitespeed`, `test:searchwords`, `test:adminpaging`, `test:locationbuckets`.

- **Speed Insights** (`@vercel/speed-insights`, `<SpeedInsights />` in `app/layout.tsx`) collects real-user data; same-origin, no CSP change. Measurement scripts: `scripts/measure-perf.mjs` (server time), `scripts/measure-lighthouse.mjs` (Lighthouse 12.8.2 mobile), `scripts/measure-admin-loaders.ts` (admin loaders run from this machine — includes the Lahore↔Mumbai hop a bom1 function does not pay). Regions: Supabase ap-south-1 and Vercel `bom1` already match; nothing changed.
- **Paging** (`lib/adminPagingCore.ts`, `components/admin/LoadMoreLink.tsx`): the server reads `?n=` (25, 50, …; a second list on a page uses its own param — `nr`, `on`, `vn`), filters + searches + counts the whole list, sends the slice. Chip/filter links drop `n`. People: `lib/cardGridPagingCore.ts` + `OverviewCardGrid paged` (overview/[key] keeps the client grid); Applications: `lib/applicationsPagingCore.ts` `applicationsView()` (groups by tuition or tutor; Older and "viewed the number" page on their own); Follow-ups (tutors, parents, "no reply"), Paid tutors, Unmet searches, Finance (Needs attention / All good are links now; counts and cards from `SettlementTab`), Tuitions and its load-more route (25), Locations (25).
- **Caching**: landing pages and the sitemap read ONE shared live cache of `landing_combinations` (`liveCombinationsForPages`, 5 minutes, `LANDING_TAG`, cleared by `revalidateLanding()`) — the uncached view read had become the database's largest cost. `repeatOfOlderOpen` is cached 5 minutes under `TUITION_TAG` (`lib/cacheTags.ts`), cleared by `revalidateTuitions()` from `queueIndexingUpdate` / `queueIndexingForStatus`, i.e. every tuition post / edit / pause / resume / refresh / close / hire / merge. `jobByPublicSlug` and the tuition page's merge lookup are read once per request. Pages with signed-in data stay uncached.
- **Images**: CNIC / selfie / degree previews are never given to Next's optimiser (its output is public); `/api/documents/[id]/preview?w=160|320|640` returns a resized copy, still `private, no-store` (`lib/docPreviewSize.ts`); thumbnails ask for 640 and lazy-load. Ad creatives stay plain `<img>` on purpose.
- **Search words** (`INTENT_WORDS` in `lib/smartSearchCore.ts`): online / home / home tuition → job type Online Tutor / Home Tutor; morning / afternoon / evening (and Roman-Urdu forms) → time of day (tuitions: `timings` mentions it, or names none); jobs / job / vacancy → tuition jobs (Find tutors redirects to Browse tuitions); overseas / abroad / uae / dubai / saudi … → online, student abroad (one chip). "Grade 1-5" / "class 6 to 8" name every grade in the range. A time of day is dropped first when nothing is found ("No tuitions mention evening lessons yet. Showing every time of day."). `reconcileSubjectQuery` (lib/smartSearch) runs inside `browseJobs` and `rankedTutors`, so the first window and Load more agree. The 24 Oct "English" miss: a guest had picked IELTS Preparation (master 4208) in the dropdown, then typed "English"; both were required. Re-run of the 20 distinct unmet searches of the week: 12 now find results (`scripts/report-unmet-rerun.ts`, which runs the real loaders as a guest through `scripts/stubs/guest-register.mjs`).
- **Locations clean-up** (`lib/locationBucketsCore.ts`): each staff-added or typed value is bucketed auto / suggest / new area / not an area with a confidence. Auto (exact after folding spellings, or one letter away on 6+ letters, one candidate, same city) is applied by `scripts/dataop-locations-cleanup.ts --apply` as an approved spelling or a retired staff area, one `location.auto_merge` audit row each; Locations → "Automatic merges" lists them with an Owner-only Undo (`/api/admin/locations/auto-merges`, `location.auto_merge_undo`) that stamps rows back, never deletes. Suggestions show a one-click "Approve: …" and "Approve all suggestions (N)". Typo'd city values are bucketed but never applied (no undo without a delete).
- **Share on WhatsApp** (`lib/profileShareCore.ts` `fillShareMessage` / `shareMessageHref`, `lib/profileShare.ts` `withShareMessages`): template `profile_share` (Team inbox → Templates → Share profile; `{tutor_name}`, `{city}`, `{subjects}`, `{profile_link}`), "verified" left out for a tutor without the Verified badge, up to three subjects (schools never), the staff ref on the link.
- **Content queue**: the page asks Google again whenever the stored Search Console result is an error or missing (`needsFreshCheck`), so an old error is never shown; "Rebuild now" already read Google first. On 28 Oct the API answered with 764 queries. "Other topics" list tutor-side topics (for tutors, or Tutor career) first (`tutorSideFirst`).

## Deep scan and clean-up, parent WhatsApp everywhere, Backend Support, single-Owner lock (owner, 31 Oct 2026)

Migration 215 (dry-run then applied before the code; `scripts/apply-migration-215.ts` proves every refused Owner change in a rolled-back savepoint). Later-dated than everything above, so under precedence rule 10 it wins where it touches them: **Bulk import is retired** (supersedes "Bulk tutor import" and every `/admin/import` note); **Pay later, the Privacy (consent number) switch, the consent chip/box and the PayPro/AssanPay health and fee cards are gone from every screen**; **a parent WhatsApp is ALWAYS their situation message** (supersedes the 27 Oct "plain help message" fallback); **there is exactly one Owner** (rule 15). `npm run test:deepscan`; read-only `scripts/report-inventory-usage.ts` (the 30-day inventory).

- **Single-Owner lock** (rule 15). `staffChangeProblem` refuses `newRole 'owner'` for every actor (the Owner included) and any role change/removal of the Owner (`OWNER_ROLE_FIXED`); a two-factor reset is not a role change. The Team route refuses `adminRole 'owner'` for everyone. Migration 215: unique index on `admin_role = 'owner'`; trigger `guard_single_owner` refuses an Owner insert, any change to/from Owner, suspending, banning, merging, marking former staff and deleting the Owner (the auth user's delete cascades into it).
- **Bulk import retired.** Page, API and `lib/import.ts` removed; `/admin/import`, `/admin/bulk-import` (and sub-paths) 308 to `/admin` in `next.config.ts`; the `import` SCREEN_ACCESS key and nav item are gone. Imported profiles, the `imported`/`claimed_at` columns and the claim flow are unchanged.
- **PayPro/AssanPay/Pay later/consent UI.** Removed: the gateway health and "PayPro service fee" cards and the retired line (Payment gateways is the bank-transfer switch and bank details only; its route accepts only `method 'bank_transfer'`), the tutor dashboard's PayPro "Complete your payment" prompt, every Pay later link (`CheckoutMethods` has no `payLater`), Settings → Privacy (`/admin/settings/privacy` 308 to `/admin`; the stored switch stays off and the reveal check still reads it), the consent chip/box, the consent routes and the consent column/report. Kept until the 25 Nov 2026 cut-off: `/paypro/uis`, the reconcile cron, `/admin/finance/paypro` and the settlement board. Consent DATA (`tuition_consents`) is kept.
- **Retired templates** (`lib/templateRetireCore.ts`; migration 215 `retired_at`/`retired_reason`, `active=false`, never deleted): job_under_review, payment_received, payment_approved, consent_ask, signup_email_unconfirmed, signup_profile_unfinished. Team inbox → Templates shows them under a collapsed "Retired (n)"; they are never offered in compose, never reviewed, and `sendAdminMessage` refuses one (410). No exact duplicate bodies existed. The compose box also never offers the screen-filled templates (pf_*, app_*, fu_*, seeded_tuition_live, applicants_forward, profile_share, status_image_verified, city_hiring_post).
- **Parent WhatsApp** (`lib/parentWhatsApp.ts` `sendParentMessage` is the one builder: the pf_* template for the parent's situation — no tuition, no applicants, tutors applied, email not confirmed, tuition ended, tutor hired — with its link on its own line and the staff ref). `/api/admin/whatsapp` never falls back to a generic text for a parent (an error in words instead); `adminMemberMessage` no longer builds parents; My sign-ups / Team activity parent rows open the situation message; the member page's payment follow-up is tutors-only; the pf_expired repost link is the tuition's own page (never a dashboard). Declared message-specific parent sends: Applications stage messages (app_*), Forward to parents (applicants_forward), the team-posted tuition contact (seeded_tuition_live — now WhatsApp number first), a parent document decision with its reason, Team inbox free text. `scripts/test-deep-scan.ts` WA_REGISTRY fails on any undeclared staff WhatsApp builder. The tutor home-address rejection now sends tutor words and a tutor link.
- **Backend Support** (`admin_role 'backend_support'`, label "Backend Support", home `/admin/follow-ups`). Writes Follow-ups (Tutors, Parents), Applications (any tuition — `mayEditStage`), Paid tutors, Team inbox replies, member notes/calls, the logged Show of a phone; views People and member pages through the new `usersView` key (every member ACTION route stays on `users`, so warn/suspend/ban/mobile are refused and hidden — `canModerate`); sees Payments status (amounts removed) and marks a receipt "Checked" (`receiptCheck`, `/api/admin/payments/receipt-check`). Never Finance, revenue, bank details, gateways, posting tuitions, merge/convert/erase, settings, roles, staff, templates or CNIC numbers. Migration 215 takes it out of `is_admin_writer`/`is_admin_full_writer` and adds RESTRICTIVE select policies so its token reads no payments, bank credits, settlement/refund/finance tables or `app_settings 'pay.%'`. Its KPI card (follow-ups today/7 days, overdue To do, red Paid tutors, forwarded within 24 hours) is at the top of Follow-ups (`lib/backendSupportKpi*`).
- **Receipt rule** (`lib/staffReceiptCore.ts`, applied in `/api/admin/payments/decide` → `lib/staffReceipt.ts`): Backend Support may APPROVE a pending bank-transfer Verification Fee receipt only when exactly one unlinked, non-PayPro bank credit is in the Fee match range, within 48 hours, with a sender name matching the member's (namesMatch), the sign-up is not credited to that staff member, the fee is not already paid and no refund is open. Everything else, and every rejection, stays Owner-only. A staff approval runs the same `activatePayment`, links that credit, stamps `payments.staff_approved_*`, audits `payment.staff_approve` (refusals `payment.staff_approve_refused`). The daily cron rings the Owner's bell with yesterday's list (`staff_approvals_daily` → `/admin/payments/staff-approved`); the Owner's Undo (`/api/admin/payments/staff-approvals`, password re-check) puts the payment back to pending, removes the badge (fee) or cancels the plan, soft-removes the credit link and audits `payment.staff_approve_undo`.
- **Small fixes:** `/messages` (the message digest email's button) now redirects to the role inbox; "Settlement check" → "Finance"; "Tutors not yet contacted"; member page "Team activity"; Follow-ups → Orphaned accounts follows the orphans list's roles; bare empty states given a next step; "Grant or revoke a plan" shown only to roles that open Plans.

## Push 1 of "Fast admin, Number first, cost and email clean-up" (owner, 31 Oct 2026)

Migrations 216 (`job_contacts.parent_account_id`; template `social_links`) and 217 (`member_follow_ups.source` may be `social_links`), each dry-run then applied before the code. Later-dated than everything above, so under precedence rule 10 it wins where it touches them: **a member's email change sends ONE TutorMint confirmation and nothing from Supabase** (supersedes "supabase.auth.updateUser({ email })", PR29 §4); **staff Post a tuition starts with the parent's number only** ("Number first"); **the tutor record page is the member page's Edit tab**; **Payments → Settings is one page**; **Email, Email usage and SMS are one Messaging page**; **Advertisements, Featured WhatsApp and staff claims are retired** (data kept); **the 5-minute cron is `/api/cron/every-5-min`**; **AssanPay, the payment simulator and six one-off internal routes are removed**. `npm run test:push1`. The owner chose three pushes with a report and an OK between each; push 2 (fast admin lists + Overview summary) and push 3 (cacheable public pages + Data Cache) follow.

- **Paired emails — cause.** Supabase's "Secure email change" mails the current sign-in address AND the new one on every `updateUser({ email })`; for a mobile sign-up the current address is the placeholder `<msisdn>@users.tutormint.org`, and re-submitting the same pending address (the onboarding email step had no double-tap guard) sent the pair again. 17–31 Oct: ~15 email changes a day (half from placeholder accounts), 265 changes pending. **Fix:** `/api/account/email` → `requestEmailChange` (lib/emailConfirm; rules lib/emailChangeCore): the new address waits on the auth user's `app_metadata.tm_pending_email` (server-written only), ONE `confirm_email` goes to it, a repeat of the same address within 60 s sends nothing, and the click (`/api/auth/confirm-email` → `completePendingChange`) sets the sign-in address (`email_confirm: true`) and `profiles.email`. The old Supabase-flow links already sent still complete through `/api/auth/callback`. Double-submit guards (`lib/submitGuard`, ref flags): RegisterForm, ForgotPassword (email + SMS), LoginForm resend (one per minute), EmailCard, EmailConfirmReminder, the onboarding email step (Next disabled while sending).
- **The 422s:** all 680 auth addresses, 265 pending changes and 675 profile addresses pass `recipientProblem` today; Resend's SMTP failures are not readable without the Resend dashboard. Our own sends already skip invalid addresses (`sendOne`/`sendBulk`) and bounce/complaint → `email_do_not_send` via the webhook (off until `RESEND_WEBHOOK_SECRET`).
- **Number first** (`lib/numberFirstCore.ts` pure, `lib/numberFirst.ts`, `POST /api/admin/jobs/number-check` — `SCREEN_ACCESS.jobsPost`, `components/forms/NumberFirstGate.tsx`, `PostTuitionForm numberFirst`). Outcomes in order: Do not use ("This number has asked not to be contacted. Please don't post it.") · On hold ("This number has a complaint on file. Please check with the owner first.") — both keep the form closed and audit `job.number_blocked` with a 12-character hash prefix, never the number · existing open/paused tuitions (TM number, title, city, status, posted date, poster; "Open it" / "Continue anyway") · a parent account ("This parent has an account (<name>)."; the tuition is linked through `job_contacts.parent_account_id`, shown on `/admin/jobs/[id]`, ownership unchanged) · clear. Masked numbers only. The edit form shows the saved number as "checked" with "Change number". Saves re-check (`doNotUseRefusal`, both lists) and re-link (`linkTuitionToParentByNumber`). The parent's own form already had both checks (`createJob` → `doNotUseRefusal`).
- **Edit tab** (`/admin/users/[id]/edit`, `SCREEN_ACCESS.tutors`; `MemberTabs showEdit`): TutorFieldEditor (name, mobile, CNIC number and pictures, photo, selfie, subjects, city, areas, profile address), change history, retired addresses. Documents are still DECIDED on the Profile tab. `/admin/tutors/<id>` 308s there (`lib/memberEditCore`); "Change mobile" is no longer on the Profile tab for a tutor.
- **Payments → Settings** (`/admin/payments/settings`, sections in `lib/paymentSettingsCore`): Fee and plans switches · Bank transfer details · Fee match range and bank email reader · PayPro settlement status. Every access list and route check unchanged (all `[]`, `requireFreshAuth`). `/admin/payments/settings/gateways`, `/admin/payments/bank-details`, `/admin/finance/settings` redirect to their section.
- **Messaging** (`/admin/settings/messaging`, `SCREEN_ACCESS.messaging` = admin; usage and SMS sections only to their own lists). `/admin/settings/email|email-usage|sms` redirect to `#email|#usage|#sms`.
- **Retired** (`lib/retiredAdminCore.ts`): page files call `permanentRedirect` (the proxy's sign-in check runs first, so a signed-out visitor goes to /login once); APIs answer 410 via `lib/retiredApi` and change nothing. Advertisements (`/admin/ads` → /admin; public house banners unchanged), Featured WhatsApp (→ /admin/follow-ups; Overview item removed), staff claims (→ /admin/team-activity; "Your claims" and the review link gone). Data kept.
- **Removed code:** `/api/internal/{blog-draft-test,link-ayaz-bank-credit,merge-duplicates,suggest-cnic-numbers,switched-tutor-email,webhook-test}` and their scripts; `lib/payments/assanpay.ts`, `lib/payments/simulator.ts`, `/pay/simulator`, `/api/payments/simulate`; `/api/payments/webhook` now answers 401 to everything; `getProvider()` is manual only. Env vars no longer read (owner to delete in Vercel): `ASSANPAY_BASE_URL`, `ASSANPAY_MERCHANT_ID`, `ASSANPAY_API_KEY`, `ASSANPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `PAYMENTS_SIMULATOR`, `PAYMENTS_SIMULATOR_SECRET`.
- **Send social links** (`lib/socialLinksCore.ts`, `POST /api/admin/follow-ups/social-links`, `components/admin/SendSocialLinksButton`): paid tutors only (fee paid), beside Send status image; one line per SOCIAL_* page that is set, `?ref=<sender's code>` on every platform but Instagram; follow-up source `social_links` with the 10-second Undo; template in Team inbox → Templates → Social links.
- **Content queue:** tutor topics first in every list (`tutorTopicsFirst`); junk Google searches (`isJunkQuery`: only single letters/numbers, a brand name alone, a word repeated back to back) are hidden topics behind "Show hidden" and never queued.
- **Logging:** per-request info logs cut 26 → 8 (OTP/SMS sends, CNIC reads, indexing accepts and every "email not sent" line removed — `sms_sends`, `email_sends`, `indexing_attempts` and the document row already record them). Raw error objects log their message only; the development email/SMS printers mask the address and number. `pg` is a devDependency.
